# Football Archive — UCL 2017-18 / existing 256 candidate tier and position-rank review

> **DRAFT — user not approved.** 2017/18 UEFA technical-report individual aggregates verified for all existing candidate names, but 2015/16 and 2016/17 all-player independent match-by-match audits are incomplete. A full three-season individual historical certification is NOT claimed.
**Latest data commit:** `80ccce0a5a7f4e9c9e16feafa20f7e9de672c206`

## Scope and comparison
- Original existing candidates: **256**; previously tiered and ranked: **193**; originally blank: **63**.
- Current draft graded and ranked: **252/256**, 4 on-field absent candidates held, no tier.
- All original player IDs, original-language names, original clubs, positions, match fixtures, group tables, club definitions and source links preserved.
- 2017/18 individual report rows mapped for **256/256**; on-pitch appearances confirmed **252**, no reported UCL on-field appearances **4**.
- Three-season conceptual weighting: **2017/18 > 2016/17 > 2015/16**. Historic 2015/16/2016/17 individual full coverage pending.
- Original UEFA official report: https://www.uefa.com/MultimediaFiles/Download/Publications/uefaorg/General/02/56/86/48/2568648_DOWNLOAD.pdf
- UEFA technical observers' season team (18 players): https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/0253-0d8222ae4584-fc81fa8bf4d6-1000--uefa-champions-league-squad-of-the-season/
- UEFA 2017/18 positional awards: https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/news/0248-0f8e632f00b8-0d24eb652107-1000--pernille-harder-and-luka-modric-voted-uefa-players-of-the/
- **DO NOT** equate 2017/18 official season aggregate row coverage with complete 3-season validation.

## Data integrity and issues
- Machine validation prior to write: 256 IDs in original order, unique; original club, original-language name and position preserved, all graded ranks unique and contiguous by position and tier-consistent, Best XI 11 valid unique candidate IDs with correct stored position.
- Birth dates still missing: **66**. Missing values left blank rather than invented.
- Original 2017/18 candidate pool represents the 16 round-of-16 clubs, not all 32 group-stage clubs.
- Name and DOB collision corrections cross-checked with club/federation records:
  - psg — Dani Alves → 다니 아우베스; date of birth 1983-05-06; https://www.fcbarcelona.com/en/football/first-team/players/4960/daniel-alves-da-silva
  - tottenham — Mousa Dembélé → 무사 뎀벨레; date of birth 1987-07-16; https://www.mlssoccer.com/players/mousa-dembele/
  - sevilla — Joaquín Correa → 호아킨 코레아; date of birth 1994-08-13; https://sevillafc.es/en/actualidad/noticias/acuerdo-entre-sevilla-fc-y-uc-sampdoria-para-el-traspaso-de-joaquin-correa
  - realmadrid — Marcelo → 마르셀루; date of birth 1988-05-12; https://www.realmadrid.com/en-US/the-club/history/football-legends/marcelo-vieira/
  - realmadrid — Casemiro → 카세미루; date of birth 1992-02-23; https://www.realmadrid.com/en-US/the-club/history/football-legends/carlos-henrique-casimiro
- UCL unplayed candidate holds: Kevin Trapp (psg); Łukasz Skorupski (roma); Simon Mignolet (liverpool); Lionel Carole (sevilla). A roster or unused-bench listing is not proof of on-field performance.
- Position scope caution: declared GK/CB/LB/RB/DM/CM/CAM/LW/RW/ST positions remain those of initial candidate file. Wing-back/multi-position players require full match-by-match checking; existing roles have not been redesigned.
- Unknown or suspect source data remain null; UEFA team profiles use blanks for zero goals/assists, not inferred for unseen player records.

- Key comparisons: Salah RW > Messi RW by remarkable 10 goals and deeper run; Mané LW has 10 goals despite technical 18-player omission; Ronaldo ST leads 15 goals and sustained prior seasons; Firmino ST provides 10 goals/8 assists but cannot be added without removing Ronaldo under one-ST 4-3-3.
